SECTION 1: EXECUTIVE ARCHITECTURE & REPOSITORY BLUEPRINT
================================================================================

1.1 CORE TECHNOLOGY STACK & RUNTIME ECOSYSTEM
- Runtime: Node.js (v18+ LTS recommended). Pure native HTTP server with zero heavyweight frameworks.
- Client: Pure Vanilla HTML5, CSS3, and ES6+ JavaScript. No React, Vue, or Angular bundle bloat.
  This ensures instantaneous load times under 500ms on mobile devices and 60 FPS tap responsiveness.
- Graphics: HTML5 2D Canvas context utilized for interactive scratch card foil erasing (scratch.js)
  and physics-driven rotary wheel deceleration (spin.js).
- Procedural Audio: HTML5 Web Audio API (AudioContext) procedural synthesis. Sound effects (square, sine,
  sawtooth waves) are generated dynamically with custom ADSR envelopes, eliminating audio asset downloads.
- Telegram Mini App: Native Telegram WebApp SDK (telegram-web-app.js) integration for automatic user context,
  theme synchronization, and native mobile haptic feedback.
- Monetization: Monetag Rewarded Interstitial SDK (Zone 11677609) with automated fallback to an internal
  5-second simulated ad countdown player when adblockers or network restrictions occur.
- Dual Cloud Database: Bidirectional synchronization with Google Firebase Realtime Database and Cloud
  Firestore, backed by local Node.js in-memory caching and localStorage persistence.

1.3 MODULAR COMPONENT LIFECYCLE & THE ASSEMBLE ENGINE (frontend/assemble.js)
Each page is developed across 3 files: <page>.html, <page>.css, and <page>.js.
The assemble script (node frontend/assemble.js):
  1. Verifies that all 3 files exist for each of the 27 pages.
  2. Injects meta tags, SDK scripts, fonts, and stylesheets into the compiled <head>.
  3. Concatenates all 27 page HTML fragments into the <main> container.
  4. Injects persistent header pills and floating cyber bottom navigation.
  5. Concatenates all shared and page JavaScript scripts into frontend/index.html.

1.4 ADMIN ASSEMBLY ENGINE (admin/assemble.js)
Compiles the 9 administrative modules into admin/index.html with top navigation tabs and administrative controls.

================================================================================
SECTION 2: BACKEND SERVER ARCHITECTURE (server.js)
================================================================================

2.1 ARCHITECTURE, DEPENDENCIES & PORT BINDING
The file server.js is the primary Node.js HTTP server. It is implemented without external
framework dependencies like Express or Fastify to minimize overhead, cold-start latency,
and memory usage in containerized or low-cost hosting environments.
- Native Dependencies: 'http', 'fs', 'path', 'url'.
- Port Resolution: Defaults to process.env.PORT or port 3000.
- Listener Creation: http.createServer(async (req, res)) processes all requests.

2.2 MIME TYPES & STATIC ASSET DELIVERY PIPELINE
The server defines a strict MIME_TYPES dictionary mapping extensions to Content-Type headers:
  - .html : 'text/html; charset=utf-8'
  - .css  : 'text/css; charset=utf-8'
  - .js   : 'application/javascript; charset=utf-8'
  - .json : 'application/json; charset=utf-8'
  - .png  : 'image/png'
  - .jpg / .jpeg : 'image/jpeg'
  - .svg  : 'image/svg+xml'
  - .webp : 'image/webp'
  - .ico  : 'image/x-icon'

Path Sanitization & Security against Directory Traversal:
  - Input URL pathname is decoded and normalized via path.normalize().
  - Traversal characters like '../' and '..\' are blocked by resolving against ROOT.
  - Root path '/' is mapped to 'frontend/index.html'.
  - Admin path '/admin' or '/admin/' is mapped to 'admin/index.html'.
  - If file exists, fs.createReadStream(filePath).pipe(res) streams bytes directly to client.
  - If file does not exist, returns HTTP 404 with text/plain '404 Not Found'.

2.3 IN-MEMORY DATA STORE (memoryStore) SCHEMA
To ensure uninterrupted operation even when Firebase connection is lost or during rapid development,
server.js maintains an internal memoryStore holding active application state:
  - memoryStore.rewards: Array of system rewards and prizes.
  - memoryStore.requests: Array of cashout and prize redemption claims from users.
  - memoryStore.users: Dictionary indexed by user UID containing player profile and balance objects.
  - memoryStore.websiteTasks: Array of partner website click and browse tasks.
  - memoryStore.telegramTasks: Array of Telegram channel and group subscription tasks.
  - memoryStore.gameConfig: Global system settings { maintenanceMode: false, announcement: '' }.
  - memoryStore.levelsConfig: Custom overrides for Levels 1 to 1000.
  - memoryStore.notifications: In-game inbox notifications array { id, type, title, message, timestamp, read, actionText, actionTarget }.
  - memoryStore.antiFraud: Real-time anti-fraud engine cache:
      * antiFraud.processedNonces: Map of cryptographic UUID nonces -> timestamps preventing duplicate claims.
      * antiFraud.userCooldowns: Map of UID -> { [action]: timestamp } enforcing rate limits per action.
      * antiFraud.auditLog: Sliding window log (latest 100 entries) capturing IP, user-agent, action, payout, and risk state.
      * antiFraud.tapRates: Velocity metrics recording tap bursts to detect bot patterns.
  - memoryStore.identityIndex: Critical uniqueness lookup tables preventing multi-accounting:
      * identityIndex.telegram: Map of normalized tgId -> UID.
      * identityIndex.phone: Map of normalized phone -> UID.
      * identityIndex.email: Map of normalized email -> UID.
  - memoryStore.authConfig: Toggles for account uniqueness rules and allowed registration methods.
  - memoryStore.accountRequests: Pending user account resolution and identity binding claims.
  - memoryStore.fuelCellsConfig: Reactor cell configurations and purchase rules.

2.4 UTILITY & HELPER FUNCTIONS IN server.js
1. normalizeTelegramId(id):
   - Lines: ~35-42
   - Parameters: id (string|number)
   - Logic: Converts id to string, removes whitespace, strips leading '@' if present.
   - Return: Sanitized string ID or empty string.

2. normalizePhone(phone):
   - Lines: ~44-52
   - Parameters: phone (string)
   - Logic: Strips all non-numeric characters (+, -, spaces, parentheses).
   - Return: Clean numeric string representation of phone number.

3. normalizeEmail(email):
   - Lines: ~54-61
   - Parameters: email (string)
   - Logic: Trims whitespace, casts to lowercase.
   - Return: Lowercased sanitized email string.

4. parseRequestBody(req):
   - Lines: ~65-80
   - Parameters: req (IncomingMessage)
   - Logic: Buffers chunks of incoming request data via 'data' events up to 10MB safety limit.
     On 'end' event, attempts JSON.parse(body). If JSON is invalid, returns empty object {}
     safely without throwing unhandled exceptions.
   - Return: Promise resolving to parsed payload object.

5. sendJson(res, statusCode, data):
   - Lines: ~82-95
   - Parameters: res (ServerResponse), statusCode (number), data (object)
   - Logic: Sets HTTP status code, Content-Type: application/json; charset=utf-8,
     and CORS headers: Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *, Access-Control-Allow-Methods: GET, POST, OPTIONS, DELETE,
     and writes JSON.stringify(data).

2.5 REST API ENDPOINTS DETAILED WALKTHROUGH
1. GET /api/health
   - Returns { status: 'ok', timestamp: Date.now(), uptime: process.uptime() }.
   - Used by load balancers, monitoring daemons, and client connection checks.

2. GET & POST /api/rewards
   - GET: Returns memoryStore.rewards array.
   - POST: Admin adds or updates a reward item. Validates title, cost, and stock.

3. GET & POST /api/requests
   - GET: Returns pending prize redemption and Level 1000 Cash Prize claims.
   - POST: Player submits a new payout request with user details, payout method (UPI/Paytm), and amount.

4. GET & POST /api/users
   - GET: Lists users with pagination and search query filtering by name, UID, or Telegram ID.
   - POST: Admin modifies user balance or toggles isBanned status.

5. GET & POST & DELETE /api/tasks/website & /api/tasks/telegram
   - GET: Returns active list of engagement tasks.
   - POST: Admin creates a new task with title, url, rewardCoins, rewardKeys, and timerSeconds.
   - DELETE: Removes task by ID.

6. GET & POST /api/game-config
   - GET: Returns global configuration: { maintenanceMode, announcement, appTitle }.
   - POST: Admin updates maintenance mode or emergency broadcast banner.

7. GET & POST /api/levels-config
   - GET: Returns custom configurations for Levels 1 to 1000.
   - POST: Admin overrides XP required and reward drops for any level between 1 and 1000.

8. POST /api/auth/telegram
   - Authenticates Telegram Mini App launch data.
   - Checks memoryStore.identityIndex.telegram for existing binding.
   - If new user, creates user record and registers telegram ID into index.
   - Returns user profile and session token.

9. GET & POST /api/auth/check-identifier
   - Checks if a phone number, email, or Telegram ID is already bound to another account.
   - Prevents duplicate account creation and multi-accounting exploitation.

10. POST /api/auth/link-credential
   - Binds a secondary identifier (e.g. phone or email) to an existing authenticated user profile.
   - Enforces unique indexing to ensure an identifier cannot be linked twice.

11. GET & POST /api/auth/config
   - Manages authorization policies (e.g. requirePhoneVerification, allowGuestMode).

12. GET /api/auth/duplicates & POST /api/auth/resolve-duplicate
   - Scans user database for conflicting credentials and allows admin to merge or unlink accounts.

13. GET & POST /api/account_requests
   - Handles appeals and identity claim tickets submitted by players.

14. POST /api/auth/migrate-indexes
   - Diagnostic utility that scans existing user records and rebuilds identityIndex hash maps.

15. GET & POST /api/notifications & /api/notifications/mark-read
   - GET: Fetches active notifications for player's in-game inbox.
   - POST /api/notifications: Admin broadcast dispatcher sending Global, Event, Maintenance, or Reward alerts.
   - POST /api/notifications/mark-read: Updates read status for single or all notifications.

16. POST /api/reward/claim (CRITICAL SERVER-AUTHORITATIVE REWARD VALIDATION)
   - Validates user identity, nonces, action cooldowns, and mathematical score boundaries.
   - Rejects replay attacks (HTTP 409) if a nonce was previously processed.
   - Enforces minimum cooldowns: Memory Match (12s), Coin Catcher (15s), Ads (8s), Streak (1hr).
   - Validates score caps and calculates authoritative coins/diamonds on server, updating memoryStore.users[uid].
   - Returns authoritative player balance to client to overwrite local optimistic values.

17. POST /api/anti-fraud/verify-tap-session (TAP VELOCITY & AUTOCLICKER DETECTION)
   - Evaluates tap frequency: ratePerSec = tapCount / durationSeconds.
   - Automatically blocks impossible bot frequencies (>40 taps/sec) with HTTP 400 and logs security audit.

18. GET & POST /api/fuel-cells-config & /api/fuel/buy
   - Server-authoritative fuel cell configuration and purchasing engine.

================================================================================
SECTION 3: FRONTEND CORE & SHARED CLIENT SERVICES (frontend/shared/)
================================================================================

3.1 REACTIVE GAME STATE & STORAGE ENGINE (frontend/shared/state.js)
The file state.js is the central state management core of the client application. It maintains
window.gameState as the single source of truth and manages persistence with localStorage and cloud.

A. DETAILED SCHEMA OF window.gameState:
1. player: Object containing core account and currency balances:
   - uid: String. Unique identifier (Telegram ID or generated UUID).
   - name: String. Display name of the user.
   - level: Number. Current player level from 1 to 1000.
   - xp: Number. Accumulated lifetime experience points.
   - coins: Number. Standard gold coins earned from tapping and mini-games.
   - blueCoins: Number. Rare premium currency used in the Cyber Hatchery and shop.
   - diamonds: Number. Gem currency for special store items.
   - chestKeys: Number. Keys consumed to open Bronze, Silver, Gold, and Cyber mystery chests.
   - chestTickets: Number. Spin tickets consumed to rotate the Lucky Wheel.
   - scratchCards: Number. Cards required to unlock and scratch Quantum Scratch Tickets.
   - eggs: Number. Egg coins required to hatch eggs in the 12-Egg Cyber Hatchery.
   - isBanned: Boolean. Ban status flag.
   - referralCode: String. Unique referral code.
   - referredBy: String. UID of referring player.
   - referralCount: Number. Total players invited.

2. reactor: Object managing the central quantum tap mechanics:
   - currentEnergy: Number. Available reactor power (0 to maxEnergy).
   - maxEnergy: Number. Total energy capacity (scales with generator upgrades).
   - clickPower: Number. Energy consumed and coins awarded per tap.
   - regenRate: Number. Energy restored per second (e.g. 3/s).
   - lastEnergyTimestamp: Number. Unix timestamp of last energy state calculation.

3. energyGenerator: Object managing the 7-tier fuel injector:
   - level: Number. Generator level.
   - remainingSeconds: Number. Turbo boost countdown timer.
   - boostMultiplier: Number. Multiplier applied to energy regen during turbo boost.
   - fuelCells: Object containing counts for 7 colored fuel cells:
     { green: 0, darkgreen: 0, yellow: 0, orange: 0, red: 0, pink: 0, purple: 0 }.

4. progression: Object managing global level milestones:
   - activeLevel: Number. Active milestone level (1 to 1000).
   - completedLevels: Object. Hash map of claimed level rewards { [lvl]: true }.
   - levelXp: Number. Current XP accumulated toward the next level.

5. goalState: Object managing 3-emoji milestone roadmap (Goals 1 to 1000):
   - currentLevel: Number. Current goal milestone (1 to 1000).
   - currentSubtab: String. Active subview ('goals' | 'mega').
   - levelProgress: Object. Collected items for active goal: { cards: 0, keys: 0, tickets: 0 }.
   - claimedGoals: Object. Hash map of completed goals { [lvl]: true }.
   - megaWatchedAds: Number. Rewarded ads watched toward Level 1000 Mega Goal Reward (0 to 1000).
   - megaRewardClaimed: Boolean. True if Level 1000 Mega Goal Reward was claimed.

6. xpState: Object managing XP level rewards (Levels 1 to 1000):
   - currentSubtab: String. Active subview ('levels' | 'mega').
   - claimedLevels: Object. Hash map of claimed level rewards { [lvl]: true }.
   - watchedAds: Number. Rewarded ads watched toward Level 1000 Mega Cash Prize (0 to 1000).
   - megaRewardClaimed: Boolean. True if 10,000 Coins Level 1000 Cash Prize was claimed.
   - seasonEndMs: Number. Timestamp marking current seasonal leaderboard end.

7. eggHatchState: Object managing the 12-Egg Cyber Hatchery:
   - eggs: Array. 12 egg objects with { id, revealed, itemType, rewardDef }.
   - collected: Object. Collection meter tallies: { key: 0, ticket: 0, card: 0, coin: 0, blueCoin: 0 }.
   - hatchedCount: Number. Number of eggs hatched in active round (0 to 11).
   - targetWinner: String. Category selected to win on the 11th hatch ('key'|'ticket'|'card'|'coin'|'blueCoin').
   - first10Items: Array. Shuffled pool of 10 items (2 of each category) ensuring no 3-match occurs early.
   - gameCompleted: Boolean. True when 11th hatch celebration modal is triggered.

8. singleCardState: Object managing Quantum Scratch Ticket:
   - hasActiveTicket: Boolean. Strict card lock flag. When false, canvas cannot be scratched.
   - currentReward: Object. Secret prize object revealed underneath the foil.
   - isRevealed: Boolean. True when >= 48% foil is cleared.

B. FUNCTIONS IN state.js:
1. getDefaultGameState():
   - Returns fresh state object populated with default baseline values.
2. saveGame():
   - Serializes window.gameState to JSON and writes to localStorage under key 'energyTapSaveData'.
   - Calls debounced cloud sync (saveToCloudDebounced()) to replicate state to Firebase.
3. loadGame():
   - Reads 'energyTapSaveData' from localStorage.
   - Performs deep recursive merge with getDefaultGameState() so newly added fields are never undefined.
   - Calls calculateOfflineEnergy() to restore energy accumulated while the user was away.
4. calculateOfflineEnergy():
   - Computes elapsedSeconds = (Date.now() - reactor.lastEnergyTimestamp) / 1000.
   - Calculates restoredEnergy = elapsedSeconds * reactor.regenRate.
   - Caps currentEnergy at maxEnergy and updates lastEnergyTimestamp.
5. resetGame():
   - Clears localStorage and reinitializes gameState to defaults.
6. claimServerAuthoritativeReward(action, payload):
   - Core security bridge enforcing server authority over game economy.
   - Generates unique UUID nonce (nonce_timestamp_random) preventing replay attacks.
   - Posts claim payload to /api/reward/claim for server validation (cooldowns & mathematical bounds).
   - Adopts verified server balances for player coins, diamonds, and keys directly into gameState.
   - If offline or network unavailable, queues claim and safely syncs upon reconnect.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
3.2 APPLICATION CONTROLLER, ROUTER & IN-GAME NOTIFICATION CENTER (frontend/shared/app.js)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
The file app.js serves as the master orchestrator, router, audio synthesizer, and notification center.

A. DOM NODE CACHING (DOM):
To prevent DOM query thrashing during 60 FPS tap animations, app.js caches frequently accessed
elements at initialization:
  - DOM.headerLevel, DOM.headerCoins, DOM.headerDiamonds, DOM.headerEnergy: Header pill counters.
  - DOM.headerInboxBtn, DOM.unreadBellDot: In-game inbox bell icon and pulsating red unread badge dot.
  - DOM.notificationModalOverlay, DOM.notificationItemsList: Notification sheet overlay and card feed.
  - DOM.appMain: Master viewport container holding all 27 page views.
  - DOM.bottomNav: Floating cyber navigation bar.

B. PAGE NAVIGATION ENGINE (switchPage(pageId)):
  - Parameter: pageId (string). E.g. 'home', 'scratch', 'egg', 'memoryMatch', 'coinCatcher', 'xp', 'goal'.
  - Step 1: Removes 'active' class from all .page-view elements.
  - Step 2: Capitalizes pageId to find container #page<CapitalizedId> (e.g. #pageMemoryMatch, #pageCoinCatcher).
  - Step 3: Adds 'active' class to target container, triggering CSS opacity transition.
  - Step 4: Updates active state on bottom navigation bar icons.
  - Step 5: Triggers lifecycle entry hooks if defined by the target page (e.g. initMemoryMatch(),
            initCoinCatcher(), initScratchPage(), renderEggPageContent(), renderGoalsList(), renderLevelsList()).
  - Step 6: Plays subtle page transition audio via sfx.playTapSound(440).

C. IN-GAME NOTIFICATION CENTER INBOX SUBSYSTEM:
  - fetchNotifications(): Queries GET /api/notifications; counts unread items; toggles #unreadBellDot pulse.
  - toggleNotificationInbox(show): Displays or dismisses sliding bottom-sheet modal (#notificationModalOverlay).
  - filterNotifications(category): Filters feed by 'all', 'reward', or 'system' with active filter pill styling.
  - renderNotifications(): Generates styled cards with title, relative timestamp, message, type badge, and CTA button.
  - markAllNotificationsRead(): Submits POST /api/notifications/mark-read { all: true } and clears unread indicators.
  - openNotificationTarget(target): Closes inbox modal and routes player directly to target page view.

D. PROCEDURAL WEB AUDIO SYNTHESIZER (window.sfx):
Built using HTML5 Web Audio API (AudioContext). Eliminates network lag and external audio file requests.
  1. playTapSound(frequency = 520):
     - Creates OscillatorNode with 'triangle' waveform and GainNode.
     - Plays ultra-short 0.04s burst with exponential decay envelope.
  2. playLevelUpSound():
     - Plays an arpeggiated 4-tone victory chord (C5: 523Hz, E5: 659Hz, G5: 784Hz, C6: 1046Hz)
       staggered by 70ms intervals with smooth decay.
  3. playCoinSound():
     - Generates high-pitch 987Hz sine wave tone simulating metallic coin pickup.
  4. playErrorSound():
     - Generates low-pitch 140Hz sawtooth wave indicating insufficient energy, coins, or cards.
  5. playWhooshSound():
     - Simulates air whoosh for spinning wheels and modal transitions using filtered noise.

E. TELEGRAM WEBAPP HAPTICS (window.triggerTelegramHaptic(type)):
Checks if window.Telegram?.WebApp?.HapticFeedback is available and invokes native device vibrations:
  - Impact feedback: 'light', 'medium', 'heavy', 'rigid', 'soft'.
  - Notification feedback: 'success', 'warning', 'error'.

F. TOAST & MODAL ENGINES:
  - showFloatingToast(message, type = 'info'): Spawns floating neon pill at top of screen.
    Automatically animates slide-down, stays for 2.8s, and fades out.
  - openTabModal(title, contentHtml): Creates centered glassmorphic dialog with backdrop blur.
  - closeTabModal(): Smoothly dismisses modal dialog.

G. MASTER UPDATE LOOP (updateUI()):
Runs on state modifications and periodic 1000ms ticker:
  - Refreshes header pill counters (Level, Coins, Diamonds, Energy).
  - Updates active page UI components.
  - Calls energy regeneration ticker.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
3.3 CLOUD DATABASE & REALTIME SYNCHRONIZATION (frontend/shared/firebase-service.js)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Manages bidirectional synchronization between local client state and Google Firebase cloud stores.
- Supported Backends: Dual support for Firebase Realtime Database (RTDB) and Cloud Firestore.
- saveToCloudDebounced(): Debounces save requests by 2500ms to avoid saturating write quotas during fast tapping.
- saveToCloudImmediate(): Flushes state immediately to /users/{uid} on critical events (level up, cashout claim).
- Offline Resilience: Attaches 'online' and 'offline' window listeners. If connection drops, mutations
  are held in a local sync queue and automatically pushed when connection recovers.
- fetchCloudData(uid): Reads player profile upon app boot. Compares cloud timestamp with local timestamp.
  If cloud data is newer, updates local state.
- syncLeaderboard(): Automatically pushes player's lifetime coin balance and level to /leaderboard collection.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
3.4 AD NETWORK INTEGRATION & SIMULATOR (frontend/shared/ad-service.js)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Integrates Monetag Rewarded Interstitial advertising with client-side fraud prevention.
- Official Monetag Zone ID: 11677609.
- showRewardedAd(onSuccess, onFail):
  Step 1: Checks if Monetag SDK function window.show_11677609 exists.
  Step 2: If available, triggers ad overlay. On successful view completion, triggers onSuccess().
  Step 3: If blocked by adblocker, network timeout, or SDK failure, automatically falls back
          to startAdSimulation() so the player is NEVER stuck or prevented from leveling up.
- startAdSimulation(zoneId, title, desc, onComplete):
  Displays an in-app simulated ad player modal with a high-tech progress countdown (typically 5s),
  ensuring game continuity in offline testing environments.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
3.5 GLOBAL DESIGN SYSTEM & CYBER THEME TOKENS (frontend/shared/common.css)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Defines the cyberpunk visual styling and layout tokens:
- Obsidian Space Backgrounds: --bg-primary: #040919, --bg-card: rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.75).
- Neon Energy Accents: --emerald: #10b981, --cyan: #06b6d4, --purple: #8b5cf6, --gold: #fbbf24.
- Glassmorphism: backdrop-filter: blur(16px), border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08).
- Typography: 'Plus Jakarta Sans' for primary UI headings, 'JetBrains Mono' for numbers and crypto counters.
- Animation Keyframes: @keyframes pulse-glow, @keyframes spin-slow, @keyframes float-up.

================================================================================
SECTION 5: ADMIN PORTAL MODULES & CONTROL CENTER (admin/)
================================================================================

5.1 ADMIN ARCHITECTURE & MODULAR PIPELINE
The admin portal is a high-security internal dashboard accessible at /admin.
It uses the same modular architecture as the player application:
  - Compiled by admin/assemble.js from 9 distinct module directories in admin/pages/.
  - Employs administrative Firebase bridge (admin/shared/firebase.js) for cloud operations.
  - Features high-contrast dark cybersecurity aesthetics (admin/shared/common.css).

================================================================================
SECTION 6: SECURITY RULES & CONFIGURATIONS
================================================================================

6.1 FIREBASE REALTIME DATABASE SECURITY (database.rules.json)
The Realtime Database enforces strict role-based access rules:
  - /users/$uid: Enforces that read and write operations are strictly permitted only
    if auth.uid === $uid. No user can read or overwrite another player's currency balances.
  - /leaderboard: Publicly readable (.read: true) so all players can view global rankings.
    Write operations are restricted to server-authenticated tokens or verified user score submissions.
  - /system: Administrative configuration node. Read-only for players; write restricted to admin auth.

6.2 CLOUD FIRESTORE SECURITY (firestore.rules)
Firestore rules enforce granular object validations:
  - match /databases/{database}/documents
  - match /users/{userId}: allow read, write: if request.auth != null && request.auth.uid == userId;
  - match /settings/{document=**}: allow read: true; allow write: if request.auth.token.admin == true;
  - match /payout_requests/{requestId}: allow create: if request.auth != null; allow read, update: if request.auth.token.admin == true;

6.3 CLOUD HOSTING & REWRITES (firebase.json & vercel.json)
  - Asset Caching: Static assets (images, web fonts, CSS) have Cache-Control headers set to
    'public, max-age=31536000, immutable' for instant repeat-visit rendering.
  - SPA Routing Rewrites: Any route not matching a physical file is rewritten to '/index.html'
    to enable client-side navigation without 404 errors.
  - API Proxying: Calls to /api/* are routed to serverless Node.js functions on Vercel.

================================================================================
SECTION 7: END-TO-END EXECUTION FLOWS & ALGORITHMIC PROOFS
================================================================================

7.1 TAP EXECUTION & POWER CONSUMPTION FLOW
  Step 1: User touches Quantum Reactor Orb (#reactorOrb).
  Step 2: Pointer event is intercepted by handleOrbTap(event) in home.js.
  Step 3: Validates reactor power: if gameState.reactor.currentEnergy < clickPower, plays error
          sawtooth tone and shows 'Core Depleted' floating alert.
  Step 4: Energy Deduction: gameState.reactor.currentEnergy -= clickPower.
  Step 5: Resource Award: gameState.player.coins += clickPower, gameState.player.xp += clickPower.
  Step 6: Particle Generation: Spawns floating neon number at clientX/clientY coordinates.
  Step 7: Feedback: Invokes sfx.playTapSound() and window.triggerTelegramHaptic('light').
  Step 8: Saves game state locally and queues debounced cloud sync.

7.2 XP LEVEL ADVANCE & DARK GREEN FUEL MATH (LEVELS 1 TO 1000)
  - Linear XP Curve: Required XP for level L is computed as: requiredXp = L * 1000.
    * Level 1   : 1,000 XP
    * Level 500 : 500,000 XP
    * Level 1000: 1,000,000 XP
  - Dark Green Fuel Payout Formula (xp.js):
    * fuelCellsAwarded = Math.max(1, Math.min(200, Math.ceil(lvl / 5)))
    * Mathematical Progression:
      - Levels 1 to 5   : 1 Dark Green Fuel Cell
      - Levels 6 to 10  : 2 Dark Green Fuel Cells
      - Level 50        : 10 Dark Green Fuel Cells
      - Level 500       : 100 Dark Green Fuel Cells
      - Level 1000      : 200 Dark Green Fuel Cells (Max Cap)
  - Compulsory Video Ad Requirement:
    * Claiming any level reward requires watching exactly 1 rewarded ad via showRewardedAd().
  - Level 1000 Mega Cash Prize (10,000 Coins):
    * Unlocks when player reaches Level 1000.
    * Player watches 1,000 rewarded ads (tracked via xpState.watchedAds).
    * Upon reaching 1,000/1,000 ads, player submits payout claim for 10,000 Coins.

7.3 GOAL MILESTONE PROGRESSION & EXPONENTIAL SCALING (GOALS 1 TO 1000)
  - 3-Emoji Progression Model: Requires Cards 🃏, Keys/Dandiyas 🥢, and Flowers/Tickets 🌸.
  - Mathematical Scaling Formula (goal.js):
    * progressRatio = (lvl - 1) / 999
    * curveFactor = Math.pow(progressRatio, 1.8)
    * cardsRequired   = Math.floor(20 + (200000 - 20) * curveFactor)
    * keysRequired    = Math.floor(50 + (500000 - 50) * curveFactor)
    * ticketsRequired = Math.floor(35 + (350000 - 35) * curveFactor)
  - Milestones:
    * Level 1   : 20 Cards, 50 Keys, 35 Tickets
    * Level 500 : ~54,000 Cards, ~135,000 Keys, ~94,500 Tickets
    * Level 1000: 200,000 Cards, 500,000 Keys, 350,000 Tickets
  - Level 1000 Mega Goal Prize:
    * Unlocks after completing Goal Level 1000 and watching 1,000 rewarded ads.
    * Awards Grand Loot: 100 Dandiyas/Keys, 75 Cards, 150 Flowers/Tickets, and 1,000 Coins.

7.4 SCRATCH CARD CANVAS ALPHA-SUBSAMPLING MATH & 5-TIER PROBABILITIES
  - Strict Card Lock Enforcement:
    * singleCardState.hasActiveTicket must be true to scratch.
    * Without spending a card, clicking or dragging on the canvas is strictly blocked.
  - Canvas Erasing Physics (scratch.js):
    * Sets ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out'.
    * Draws overlapping circles of radius 24px along the drag path.
  - Sub-sampling Alpha Check Algorithm (checkScratchCompletion()):
    * Instead of inspecting all 300x180 = 54,000 pixels (which would cause mobile frame drops),
      the engine sub-samples every 16th pixel (stride = 64 bytes in Uint8ClampedArray).
    * If transparent pixel count (alpha === 0) reaches >= 48% of total sample, triggers auto-reveal.
  - Strict 5-Tier Prize Distribution (SCRATCH_CARD_TIERS):
    * Tier 1: 1-2 Keys           -> 20% Probability
    * Tier 2: 10-20 Eggs         -> 50% Probability
    * Tier 3: 1-2 Tickets        -> 20% Probability
    * Tier 4: 10 Coins           ->  2% Probability
    * Tier 5: 50-75 Blue Coins   ->  8% Probability
    * Total Exact Probability    : 100%
  - On reveal completion, singleCardState.hasActiveTicket resets to false, requiring a new card.

7.5 12-EGG HATCHERY PIGEONHOLE RANDOMIZATION & 11TH HATCH VICTORY PROOF
  - Grid Layout: 12 cyber eggs (4 columns x 3 rows). Cost: 1 Egg Coin per hatch.
  - 5 Win Categories (Collect 3 of any category to win):
    1. Keys        (3 Keys       -> Win 1 Key)
    2. Tickets     (3 Tickets    -> Win 1 Ticket)
    3. Cards       (3 Cards      -> Win 1 Card)
    4. Coins       (3 Coins      -> Win 10 Coins)
    5. Blue Coins  (3 Blue Coins -> Win 100 Blue Coins)
  - The 10-Item Randomization Rule:
    * At round initialization, the engine chooses targetWinner by weighted odds.
    * It generates a pool of 10 items consisting of exactly 2 items for each of the 5 categories:
      [Key, Key, Ticket, Ticket, Card, Card, Coin, Coin, BlueCoin, BlueCoin].
    * This 10-item pool is shuffled using the Fisher-Yates algorithm.
  - Mathematical Proof of Non-Completion in Hatches 1 to 10:
    * By the Pigeonhole Principle, since the maximum count of any category in the 10-item pool is 2,
      it is mathematically impossible for any category to reach 3 items during the first 10 hatches.
    * At hatch #10, all 5 collection meters will be at exactly 2/3 collected.
  - Guaranteed 11th Hatch Victory:
    * On the 11th hatch, the engine reveals the 3rd matching item for targetWinner.
    * That category hits 3/3, immediately triggering triggerEggWinCelebration().
    * Awards prize (e.g. 100 Blue Coins) and displays celebration modal.

7.6 ANTI-FRAUD AD VERIFICATION & MULTI-DEVICE ACCOUNT UNIFICATION
  - Monetag Ad Validation: SDK invokes callback only upon complete video playback.
  - Multi-Identity Uniqueness: server.js checks normalized identity indexes (Telegram ID, phone, email).
    If any identifier is already claimed by another UID, registration or linking is blocked.

7.7 MEMORY MATCH TIMING MATH, SPEED BONUS & STAR RATING PROOFS
  - 4 x 4 Matrix Shuffling: 16 cards comprising 8 identical emoji pairs (⚡, 💎, 🔋, 🚀, 🗝️, 🎫, 🥚, 🎴).
  - Card Flip State Engine: Tracks flippedCards array. Blocks clicks when flippedCards.length === 2 to prevent race conditions.
  - Countdown Timer Loop: 60-second window ticking via setInterval at 1000ms. If timer reaches 0, triggers gameOverMemory().
  - Multi-Factor Reward Algorithm:
    * Base Completion: 150 Coins, 1 Dungeon Key, 15 Diamonds.
    * Speed Bonus: timeBonusCoins = Math.max(0, timeRemaining) * 3.
    * Mistake Rating & Diamond Multiplier:
        - mistakes <= 2 : 3 Stars (⭐⭐⭐) & +60 Diamonds (Peak mastery bonus)
        - mistakes <= 5 : 2 Stars (⭐⭐)  & +35 Diamonds (Proficient solve)
        - mistakes > 5  : 1 Star  (⭐)   & +15 Diamonds (Standard solve)
  - Server Claim Verification: Client passes { timeRemaining, mistakes, stars } to POST /api/reward/claim.
    Server recalculates approved coins and diamonds using the exact same formula, rejecting any manipulated values.

7.8 COIN CATCHER PHYSICS, HAZARDS, COMBO MULTIPLIER & SCORE BOUNDS
  - Object Spawning Matrix: Spawns every 600ms at randomized X coordinates (5% to 95% stage width).
  - Weighted Entity Distribution:
    * Gold Energy Coin (🪙): 55% frequency, speed 3.2px/frame, +10 Coins, increments combo.
    * Blue Energy Coin (💠): 25% frequency, speed 4.0px/frame, +25 Coins, increments combo.
    * Super Star (⭐): 8% frequency, speed 5.2px/frame, +50 Coins, +0.2x combo boost.
    * Bomb Hazard (💣): 12% frequency, speed 3.5px/frame, causes damage.
  - Life & Collision System: Player begins with 3 Lives (❤️❤️❤️). Catching a bomb triggers screen-shake CSS
    and deducts 1 Life. Losing all 3 lives immediately aborts the 30-second run.
  - Progressive Combo Multiplier: Increases by 0.1x per consecutive catch up to a max cap of 2.5x.
    Hitting a bomb hazard immediately resets the combo multiplier to 1.0x baseline.
  - Physical Speed Bounds Checking: Server enforces that in a 30s session, maximum achievable score
    is physically bounded (< 1500 coins and < 50 diamonds), instantly clamping suspicious values.

7.9 IN-GAME NOTIFICATION DISPATCH, INBOX PERSISTENCE & DEEP-LINK ROUTING
  - Centralized Dispatch: Administrators compose announcements from the Admin Dashboard (admin/pages/dashboard/).
  - Inbox Notification Structure:
      { id, type, title, message, timestamp, read, actionText, actionTarget }.
  - Unread State Synchronization: App header polls or checks /api/notifications. If any unread message exists,
    the unread indicator pulse (#unreadBellDot) animates via CSS keyframes.
  - Contextual Deep-Link Routing:
    * Action buttons route players directly to specific game subsystems (e.g. actionTarget: 'streak'
      routes to #pageStreak; 'profile' routes to #pageProfile; 'reward' routes to #pageReward).
  - Single or Batch Mark-as-Read: Updates both server memoryStore and local storage to prevent duplicate alerts.

7.10 ANTI-FRAUD ENGINE: REPLAY PREVENTION, COOLDOWNS & AUTOCLICKER ANOMALY DETECTION
  - Cryptographic Nonce Validation: Every reward claim generates a random UUID nonce. The server maintains
    memoryStore.antiFraud.processedNonces. If a claim arrives with an existing nonce, the server flags a Replay
    Attack and returns HTTP 409 Conflict without modifying user balances.
  - Granular Per-Action Cooldown Intervals (MIN_COOLDOWNS):
    * mini_game_memory : 12,000 ms (12 seconds minimum completion time)
    * mini_game_catcher: 15,000 ms (15 seconds minimum session duration)
    * ad_reward        :  8,000 ms (8 seconds minimum viewing time)
    * daily_streak     : 3,600,000 ms (1 hour between daily claims)
    * referral_claim   :  5,000 ms (5 seconds between squad payouts)
  - Tap Velocity Anomaly Detection (/api/anti-fraud/verify-tap-session):
    * ratePerSec = tapCount / durationSeconds.
    * Human multi-finger physical limit is ~20 to 25 taps/sec.
    * Rates exceeding 40 taps/sec are physically impossible on mobile screens and indicate automated software
      autoclickers. The server blocks the session with HTTP 400 and records the incident to security auditLog.
  - Multi-Identifier Uniqueness: identityIndex maps enforce that no Telegram ID, phone number, or email
    can belong to more than one player UID, shutting down multi-account referral fraud.

7.11 SERVER-AUTHORITATIVE VS CLIENT-AUTHORITATIVE ARCHITECTURAL SEPARATION
  - Client-Authoritative (Safe UI & Visuals):
    * Active navigation tab and sub-tab selection.
    * Animation frame rendering, particle floaters, and screen-shake effects.
    * Sound effect toggles and volume preferences.
    * Selected cosmetic reactor ring skins.
    * Optimistic local counters for responsive 60 FPS tap feedback.
  - Server-Authoritative (Critical Economic Assets):
    * Gold coins, blue coins, diamonds, keys, spin tickets, and egg coins.
    * Level 1000 cashout payouts (10,000 Coins via UPI/Paytm).
    * Rewarded ad view payouts and energy refills.
    * Referral squad reward disbursements.
    * Achievement unlock certifications and badge granting.
    * Global leaderboard ranking scores.
  - Result: Player modifications to local window.gameState or localStorage cannot compromise game economics;
    any withdrawal, store purchase, or mini-game reward must be authorized by server.js.
